## Teapot Pilgrim / 1 star
{bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.04}
{bonus:MoveSpeed|Champion / Movement speed|-0.3%}
{mark} Each drink bought gives this ally +4% damage (max +40%).
{mark} Every 4s heals the hero 1.5 HP.
Owned 0/3 at 1-star

TALENT
Each DRINK purchased during this run adds 4% to this ally's weapon damage, up to +40%. Applies when the next battle is prepared; not a team-wide damage buff.
PASSIVE · Every 4s in combat, attempt to heal the hero for 1.5 HP before healing modifiers. Healing restrictions apply.
Immortal. Shown support is per body; three matching bodies merge and keep one of their weapons.
Random weapon: the attack ranges use your current build. Melee and ranged weapons are eligible.
“Need? I'm the one who asks that.”

LIFE IN THE WAITING REALM
Born above the station kitchens, they fed travellers through a winter with no departures. Every cup was counted; nobody knew whether they had eaten. They want a table where the last person serving is also given a seat.

SOURCE
Regular shop; hero theme and unlock wave apply.
Named talents count bodies and absorbed memories; star level only scales the separately listed support unless stated.

Hearth / Champion: {bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.135} every 2 bodies. Purchase unavailable; no after-purchase result shown. Extra at 4 bodies: {bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.135}. Extra at 6 bodies: {bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.27}.

Rolled up: an enemy that bumps into it is stunned for 0.3s (once every 2s per enemy).

“It uncurls now, slowly, to show what it was protecting all along.”

LIFE IN THE WAITING REALM
An armadillo found curled in stone, older than the museum that displayed it. Visitors guessed what it had been afraid of. It uncurls now, slowly, to show what it was protecting all along.

SOURCE
Recruitment exclusive: Wise
Named talents count bodies and absorbed memories; star level only scales the separately listed support unless stated.
